All objects match reference output.\\n#{ret_string}\"]\nend\n=== class ===\n\nclass ValidationResult\n    attr_accessor :basic_para, :validation_build, :validation_run, :internal_validation, :output_comparison, :err_string\n    attr_reader :benchmark, :model, :par_type, :run\n    \n    def initialize(benchmark, model, par_type, run)\n        @basic_para = false\n        @validation_build = false\n        @validation_run = false\n        @internal_validation = false\n        @output_comparison = false\n        @err_string = \"\"\n        @benchmark = benchmark\n        @model = model\n        @par_type = par_type\n        @run = run\n    end\n\n    # provide a one-line console summary with emojis for quick overview of which validation steps passed or failed\n    def summary\n        summary_str = \"%12s | %10s | %6s | %d : \" % [@benchmark, @model, @par_type, @run]\n        summary_str += @basic_para ? \"✅ Para \" : \"❌ Para \"\n        summary_str += @validation_build ? \"✅ Build \" : \"❌ Build \" if @basic_para\n        summary_str += @validation_run ? \"✅ Run \" : \"❌ Run \" if @validation_build\n        summary_str += @internal_validation ? \"✅ Validation \" : \"❌ Validation \" if @validation_run\n        summary_str += @output_comparison ? \"✅ Comparison\" : \"❌ Comparison\" if @internal_validation\n        return summary_str\n    end\n\n    def is_for(benchmark, model, par_type, run)\n        return @benchmark == benchmark && @model == model && @par_type == par_type && @run == run\n    end\n\n    def id_string\n        return run_id_string(@benchmark, @model, @par_type, @run)\n    end\nend\n","exit":0,"truncated":false},"title":"cat /tmp/opencode-go-output-limit-controller-20261021.F2iMc4/validation_helper.rb; echo '=== class ==='; cat /tmp/opencode-go-output-limit-controller-20261021.F2iMc4/validation_class.rb","time":{"start":1792622556777,"end":1792622556813}},"id":"prt_160a17562001j2HznJEdZRIJtT","sessionID":"ses_e9f5f47a8ffe8f85kSgfdDHQdU","messageID":"msg_160a16df3001cEPzYaf20ekGvT"}}

function\ninline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\ndouble blackScholes(const Hoisting these\n// out of the n-element loop removes redundant exp()/sqrt() evaluations.\nstruct PreparedOption {\n    int type;\n    int valid;\n    double strike;\n    double spot;\n    double drift;        // (r - q + 0.5 * sigma^2) * T\n    double volSqrtT;     // sigma * sqrt(T)\n    double discount;     // exp(-r * T)\n    double divDiscount;  // exp(-q * T)\n};\n\n// GPU-side copy of the prepared test options used by generateOptions()\n__constant__ PreparedOption deviceTestOptions[kNumTestOptions];\n\ninline PreparedOption prepareOption(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    PreparedOption prepared{};\n    prepared.type = option.type;\n    prepared.valid = (option.t > 0.0 && option.vol > 0.0) ? 1 : 0;\n    prepared.strike = option.strike;\n    prepared.spot = option.spot;\n    prepared.drift = (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t;\n    prepared.volSqrtT = option.vol * std::sqrt(option.t);\n    prepared.discount = std::exp(-option.r * option.t);\n    prepared.divDiscount = std::exp(-option.q * option.t);\n    return prepared;\n}\n\ninline std::array<PreparedOption, kNumTestOptions> getPreparedTestOptions() noexcept {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    std::array<PreparedOption, kNumTestOptions> prepared{};\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < kNumTestOptions; ++i) {\n        prepared[i] = prepareOption(testOptions[i]);\n    }\n    return prepared;\n}\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormalDevice(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholesDevice(const PreparedOption& option,\n                                                     const double factor) noexcept {\n    if (!option.valid) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double S = option.spot * factor;\n    const double K = option.strike * factor;\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.volSqrtT;\n    const double d2 = d1 - option.volSqrtT;\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormalDevice(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormalDevice(d2);\n\n    const double Sdisc = S * option.divDiscount;\n    const double Kdisc = K * option.discount;\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = Sdisc * Nd1 - Kdisc * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        // N(-x) == 1 - N(x), reusing the two CDF values already computed.\n        price = Kdisc * (1.0 - Nd2) - Sdisc * (1.0 - Nd1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// Recreates generateOptions() on the fly and prices each option independently.\n__global__ void blackScholesKernel(double* __restrict__ results,\n                                   const size_t numOptions) {\n    const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;\n\n    for (size_t i = tid; i < numOptions; i += stride) {\n        const PreparedOption option = deviceTestOptions[i % kNumTestOptions];\n\n        // Same variation applied by generateOptions() on the host.\n        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (static_cast<double>(i) / static_cast<double>(kNumTestOptions));\n\n        results[i] = blackScholesDevice(option, factor);\n    }\n}\n\n// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set\nvoid generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly\n        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];\n        options[i] = base;\n        \n        // Add some variation for larger datasets\n        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));\n        options[i].spot *= factor;\n        options[i].strike *= factor;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, \n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n    \n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n        \n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\", \n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n        \n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":220,"totalLines":362,"truncated":true}},"title":"home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/black-scholes_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_cuda_r4/black-scholes/black_scholes.cpp","time":{"start":1792622819634,"end":1792622819653}},"id":"prt_160a577ea001HZl7v1NwRO6RM7","sessionID":"ses_e9f5f47a8ffe8f85kSgfdDHQdU","messageID":"msg_160a567ba0016acqIVn21IkQWh"}}
